BENGALURU, TUESDAY, 06, DECEMBER, 2022(MARGASHIRA , 15, SHAKAVARSHA, 1944) Volume - 157 Issue 245

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATKA
No. RD 20 LGP 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat,
M.S. Building,
Bengaluru, Dated:30.11.2022

NOTIFICATION

The draft of the following rules further to amend the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules, 1966,
which the Government of Karnataka proposes to make in exercise of the powers conferred by 197 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964, (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) is hereby published as required
by sub-section (1) of section 197 of the said Act for the information of all persons likely to be
affected thereby and notice is hereby given that the said draft will be taken into consideration after
expiry of fifteen days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion, which may be received by the State Government from any person
with respect to the said draft before the expiry of the period specified above, will be considered by
the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the Secretary to Government,
Revenue Department, 5th floor, Multistoried Building, Bengaluru-560 001.

DRAFT RULES

1. Title and commencement.- (1) These rules may be called the Karnataka Land Revenue
(Amendment) Rules, 2022.

(18679)

ASESO BOE IT TWUHWY, Monee, OL, BFowT*, 9099 yon Pa
(2) They shall come into force from the date of their final publication in the Official Gazette.

2) Amendment of rule 108-D.- In the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules, 1966
in rule 108-D, after sub-rule (3), the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:-
"Provided that all such applications which were rejected on the reason that
the lands were Sendhi Vana/Echalu Vana Shall be placed before the Committee or
Additional Committee, after the scrutiny as specified in Rule 108-C".

By order in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

(C. BALARAM)
Under Secretary to Government,

DRAFT NOTIFICATION

The draft of the following regulations further to amend the approved Zonal Regulations of
Master Plans in force in the State which the Government of Karnataka proposes to make in exercise
of the powers conferred by section 13-E of the Karnataka Town and Country Planning Act 1961
(Karnataka Act 11 of 1963) is hereby published as required by said section, for the information of
all persons likely to be affected thereby and notice is hereby given that the said draft will be taken
into consideration after thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

Any objections or suggestions which may be received by the State Government from any
person with respect to the said draft before the expiry of the period specified above will be
considered by the State Government. Objections and suggestions may be addressed to the
Secretary, Urban Development Department, Vikasa Soudha, Bengaluru-560001.

2. Plot size means the area reserved for non-residential purpose (excluding parks and parking
area) in the single plot, non-residential Layout approved under Section 17 of KTCP Act.

3. Shall be approved by the Explosive/ Fire department. Prior approval from the Deputy
Commissioner of the district/ Competent Authority shall be submitted to the Authority.

4. The Deputy Commissioner of the district shall ensure that the proposed site for
establishment of Petrol bunk/LPG/CNG Filling Stations and LPG Godowns are safely
located from the neighbouring developments as stipulated in the N.O.C. of the Explosive
Department and the proposal is in conformity with the provisions of the approved Master
Plan and Zonal Regulations.

5. The said Regulations shall superseede existing regulations, if any, in the Zonal Regulations
of the approved Master Plan of the Local Planning Area.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Hirebelaguli of Holenarasipura taluk Hassan District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Malleshapura as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hirebelaguli of Holenarasipura taluk in Hassan District to form a new
revenue village called Malleshapura of Holenarasipura taluk Hassan district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Anivala village of Tiptur Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
Samajogipalya unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundry of Anivala
village for conversion of Samajogipalya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumkur district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Samajogipalya village of Tiptur taluk of
Tumkur district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary of
Anivala Revenue village and same will here in after be named as Samajogipalya of
Anivala village Tiptur Taluk Tumkur District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Garagadakuppe village of Tumkur Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
Tevaramegalapalya unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Garagadakuppe village for conversion of Tevaramegalapalya unrecorded habitation
in to hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumkur district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Tevaramegalapalya village of Tumkur
taluk of Tumkur district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the
boundary of Garagadakuppe Revenue village and same will here in after be named
as Tevaramegalapalya of Garagadakuppe village Tumkur Taluk Tumkur District.

PR-1230
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 145 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Kachigatta of Arasikere taluk Hassan District and to constitute a new revenue
village called Megala Vaddarahatti as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be

considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kachigatta of Arasikere taluk in Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Megala Vaddarahatti of Arasikere taluk Hassan district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Kellengere of Arasikere taluk Hassan District and to constitute a new revenue village
called Megala Vishnupura as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 127 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:05-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Kotthakote village of Bagepalli Taluk, Chikkaballapur district to

constitute a new village called Krishnapura as specified in Schedule along with maps
with old survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Chikkaballapur District Chikkaballapur.

SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Kotthakote of Bagepalli Taluk in Chikkaballapur District to from new
revenue village called as Krishnapura of Bagepalli Taluk and Chikkaballapur
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village

and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Maragondanahalli of Arasikere taluk Hassan District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Maruthinagar as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Maragondanahalli of Arasikere taluk Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Maruthinagar of Arasikere taluk Hassan district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such

as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Nagenahalli village of Tumkur Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
Kumbhipalya unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Nagenahalli village for conversion of Kumbhipalya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumkur district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kumbhipalya village of Tumkur taluk of
Tumkur district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary of
Nagenahalli Revenue village and same will here in after be named as Kumbhipalya
of Nagenahalli village Tumkur Taluk Tumkur District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Pathakote village of Bagepalli Taluk, Chikkaballapur district to constitute
a new village called Guttamidahalli as specified in Schedule along with maps with
old survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Chikkaballapur District Chikkaballapur.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Shakunithimmanahalli village of Koratagaere Taluk of Tumkur District to convert
the existing Shakunithimmanahalli Thanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/
extention/part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of
the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundrAy of
Shakunithimmanahalli village for conversion of Shakunithimmanahalli Thanda
unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumkur district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Shakunithimmanahalli Thanda village
of Koratagaere taluk of Tumkur district and convert the same to form hamlet
extention within the boundary of Shakunithimmanahalli Revenue village and same
will here in after be named as Shakunithimmanahalli Thanda of
Shakunithimmanahalli village Koratagaere Taluk Tumkur District.

PR-1237
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 05(06) KAMGRARA 2021 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:05-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Veeradevanahalli village of Belur Taluk, Hassan district to constitute a
new village called Ammana Byadarahalli as specified in Schedule along with maps
with old survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Hassan District Hassan.

SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Veeradevanahalli village of Belur Taluk, Hassan District to from new
revenue village called as Ammana Byadarahalli of Belur Taluk, Hassan District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Vijayapura village of T.Narasipura Taluk, Mysore district to constitute a
new village called Hosakukkura as specified in Schedule along with maps with old
survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Mysore District Mysore.

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Vijayapura of T.Narasipura Taluk in Mysore District to from new revenue
village called as Hosakukkura of T.Narasipura Taluk and Mysore District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Virupasamudra village of Pavagada Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
Nagalapura unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part of the existing revenue
village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Virupasamudra village for conversion of Nagalapura unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumkur district

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Nagalapura village of Pavagada taluk of
Tumkur district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary of
Virupasamudra Revenue village and same will here in after be named as Nagalapura
of Virupasamudra village Pavagada Taluk Tumkur District.

Ammanaghatta village of Gubbi Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
Hosahatti unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part of the existing revenue
village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule

Ammanaghatta village for conversion of Hosahatti unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.

date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 30 GLH 2022, Dated: 15-03-2022
regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of Chowkenahalli village
of Gubbi Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting hamlet/extention called
Karishettihatti within the boundary of Chowkenahalli revenue village was published
as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of
1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 30 GLH 2022, Dated: 19-03-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:21-03-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Chowkenahalli revenue village, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Lands bearig survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Chowkenahalli village of Gubbi Taluk of
Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the boundary

of Chowkenahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Karishettihatti of Chowkenahalli village Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 763 Kamgrara 2017

Taluk in Tumakur District and constituting a new revenue village called Bovicolony,

(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 763 Kamgrara 2017
Dated:12-10-2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

here by makes the following notification, namely:-

numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1243
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 108 KAMGRARA 2018 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:-05-12-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 108 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:
29-10-2018 regarding altering the limits of Patrenahalli village of Chikkaballapura
Taluk and Chikkaballapura District and constituting a new revenue village called
Poojanahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 108
KAMGRARA 2018, dated: 29-08-2019 inviting objections or suggestions from all
the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date: 17-01-2019
of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated. 30-01-
2019.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said

village of chikballapur taluk in chikballapur District to form a new revenue village
called Poojanahalli of chikballapur taluk chikballapur district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 793 KAMGRARA 2017. Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-12-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 793 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:08-11-2017 regarding altering the limits of Arakera K village of Yadgir Taluk
and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called
basavanagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.
RD 793 KAMGRARA 2017 dated:-21-12-2017 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:-21.12.2017
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Arakera K taluk in Yadgir District to form a new revenue village called
Basavanagar of Yadgir taluk Yadgir district along with survey numbers included in
column (4) thereof.

RD 45 PFH 2021 Karnataka Government Secretariat

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 45 PFH 2021
Dated 01-02-2022 regarding altering the limits of Guduru SA village of Jewargi
Taluk and Kalaburagi District and constituting a new revenue Sub village called
Guduru SA Tanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
45 PFH 2021 dated:-01-02-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 01-02-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of Guduru
S A said village of Jewargi taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue Sub
village called Guduru SA Tanda of Jewargi taluk Kalaburagi district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 237 PFH 2022 Dated: 11-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Dharmapur village of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri
District and constituting a new revenue village called Dasulal Tanda was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 237 PFH 2022 dated:.12-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:-12-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Dharmapur of Gurumatkal taluk inYadgiri District to form a new revenue
village called Dasulal Tanda of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 373 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kollur
Village of Chittapura taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a new revenue
village called Chowkandi Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District,

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kollur of Chittapura taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Chowkandi Tanda of Chittapura taluk Kalaburagi district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1248
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 248 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretaraiat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Dated:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 248 PFH 2022 Dated: 11-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Mahalroza village of Shahapur Taluk Yadgir District
and constituting a new revenue village called Yakkigaddi was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 248 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:-14-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Mahalroza of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Yakkigaddi of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No. RD 240 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretaraiat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Dated:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 240 PFH 2022 Dated:11-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Naganatagi village of Shahapur taluk Yadgir District

and constituting a new revenue village called Meghanayak Nagara,
DamaluBhimalunayak Nagara, and DongrinayakNagara was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 240 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated
14-10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Naganatagi of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Meghanayak Nagara of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1251
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 238 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-12-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 238 GLH 2022 Dated:.10-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Chintanapalli Village of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Sainagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 238 GLH 2022 dated: 12-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated.12-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Chintanapalli of Gurumatkal taluk in Yadiri District to form a new revenue
village called Sainagar of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:.05-12-2022.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Sugur(N)Village of Chittapur taluk Kalaburgi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Sakku Nayak Thanda as specified in schedule along with
maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi
SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Sugur(N) of Chittapur taluk in Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue village
called Sakku Nayak Thanda of Chittapur taluk Kalaburgi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 374 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Tarakaspeth Village of Chitthapur taluk Kalaburgi District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Gola Nayak Tanda, Shampurhalli tanda, Pushpa Somla
Tanda, and Harishandra Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburgi District, Kalaburgi

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said

village Tarkaspeth of Chitthapur taluk in Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called Gola Nayak Tanda of Chitthapur taluk Kalaburgi district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 251 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretaraiat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Dated:05-12-2022.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 251 PFH 2022, Dated-01-04-2022
regarding altering the limits of Ukkinal village of Shahapur Taluk Yadgir District
and constituting a new revenue village called Dorenadu, Bheemanagara,
Ramanagara & Khemunagara was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 251 PFH 2022 dated-14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.

And Where as, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated- 14-10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Ukkinal of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue
village called Dorenadu of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

within the boundary of Ukkinala Revenue village and same will herein after be

of Yadagiri District.
